53b4(1): de ltar na byang chub sems pa' nyi tshe ba ma yin pa'i yi shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no//ji ltar na byangachub sems dpa' ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe  
53b5(1): na|'di la byang chub sems dpa' nyan thos kyi ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang rab tu shes/rang sangs rgyas ting nge 'dzin rnams kyangarab tu shes/byang chub sems dpa' ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang 
53b6(1): rab tu shes|de bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin rnams kyang rab tu sheso//des ting nge 'dzin gang dang /ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yulagang la nyan thos spyod pa de yang rab tu shes/ting nge 'dzin 
53b7(1): gang dang /ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gang la rang sangs rgyas spyod pa rab tu shes/ting nge 'dzin gang dang /ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gang la byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa de yang rab tu shes|
54a1(1): @#//ting nge 'dzin gang dang /ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gang la de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod pa de'ang rab tu shes te/de bzhin gshegs pas skabs phye nas/de  
54a2(1): bzhin gshegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin rab tu shes kyi/rnam par smin pas grub pa'i rang gi ye shes kyis ni ma yin no//ting nge 'dzin gzhan rnams ni (ma)par smin pas grub pa'i rag gi   
54a3(1): yi shes kyis rab tu shes te/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul gyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems pa dpa' byin gyis brlabs 
54a4(1): kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs yin zhe na/'di la byang chub sems dpa' nyan thos kyi byin gyi brlabs kyang ji lta (ba)bzhin du rab tu shes/rang sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs kyang dzi 
54a5(1): blta ba bzhin du rab tu shes/byang chub sems pa'i byin gyi brlabs kyang ji blta ba bzhin du rab tu shes na /sems can de ma yin pa gzhan dag gi lta ci smos te/de ltar na byang   
54a6(1): chub sems dpa' byin gyi brlabs kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs payin no //ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi 'phrogs pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/'di la   
54a7(1): byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes ni mu stegs can thams cad dang /bdud thams cad dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyis mi 'phrogs pa yin te/de ltar na byang chub 
54b1(1): sems dpa' mi 'phrogs pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa la rnam par lta ba'i ye shes phun sum tshogs 
54b2(1): pa yin zhe na/'di la byang chub sems dpa' ye shes yongs su dag pa dang /zhing rnyog pa med pas sems can gyi khams la rnam par lta ste/des de na sems can la la byang chub tu sems   
54b3(1): bskyed pa dag dang /la la byang chub tu sems ma bskyed pa dag dang /la las byang chub kyi sems bsgrubs pa dag dang /la la byangachub kyi sems ma bsgrubs pa dang /la la sa dang po la gnas  
54b4(1): la la sa bcu pa'i bar la gnas pa dag dang /la la mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa dag dang /la la la mngon par rdzogs pa ra sangs rgyas te chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba dag dang 
54b5(1): la la bya ba kun byas te yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba dag dang /la la nyan thos kyi theg pas yongs su mya ngan las 'das ba dag dang /la la rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pas yongs su myi  
54b6(1): ngan las 'da' ba dag dang/la la bde 'gror skye ba dag dang /la la ngan 'gror skye ba dag mthong ngo //de ltar sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa la rnam par lta ba ste/de ltar na byang chub  
54b7(1): sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyiskyed pa la rnams par lta ba'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no //ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' lhun gyis grub pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs
55a1(1): @#//pa yin zhe na /'di la byang chub sems dpa' spyod pa 'am/ ('greng ba 'am/'dug pa 'am/) 'gro ba 'am/'ong ba na lhun gyis grub cing ye shes 'byung te/'di lta ste dper na mi gnyid kyis 
55a2(1): log pa'i dbugs 'byung ba dang rngub ba dag lhun gyis 'grub cing 'byung ngo //byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes de yang de bzhin du lta'o //ye shes de yang thams cad du mi chags par 'jug ste//
55a3(1): de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' lhun gyis grub pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad kyi mtshan nyid kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs ba yin zhe na  /
55a4(1): 'di la byang chub sems dbas chos thams cad mtshan nyid mtshungs par yang rab tu shes/mtshan nyid gcig par yang rab tu shes//mtshan nyid du mar yang rab tu shes/sgyu ma'i mtshan nyid du 'ang 
55a5(1): rab tu shes/yang dag pa ma yin pa kun brtags pa'i mtshan nyid du'ang rab tu shes ste/de ltar na byang chub sems (   )dpa' chos thams cad kyi mtshan nyid kyi ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no //
55a6(1): rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dang 'jig rten las 'das pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di la byang chub sems dpa' zag pa med pa'i ye shes 'jig rten   
55a7(1): thams cad las 'das pa dang ldan pa yin te/rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten las 'das pa'i ye shes phun sum tshogs pa yin no//rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang   
55b1(1): ldan na|byang chub sems dpa' ye shes phun sum tshogs pa rnams yin no//rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' sa dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no//bcu gang zhe na 'di lta
55b2(1): ste|yang shing tshad med pa rnams yin/sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa rnams yin/gnod pa dang lan du phan 'dogs pa dang bral ba rnams yin/chos kyi sprin chen po kun zed pa rnams
55b3(1): yin|sems can thams cad kyi gnas su gyur pa rnams yin/dge ba'i chos rnams kyi sa bon gyi gzhir gyur pa rnams yin/rin po che'i snod du gyur pa rnams yin/rtsi chen po'i snod du gyur pa 
55b4(1): rnams yin/mi bsgul ba rnams yin/mi sdang ba rnams yin//rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa'-i yangs shing tshad med pa yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/sa chen po n-i thams
55b5(1): cad du yangs shing tshad med do//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang 'di lta ste/bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs kyis kun du yangs shing tshad med pa yi te/de ltar na byang chub sems
55b6(1): dpa' yangs shing tshad med do/ji ltar na byang chub sems dnga' sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa yin zhe na/rigs kyi ba 'di lta ste dper na/sa chen po na 'di ltar yo byad rnam pa de dag gis
55b7(1): sems can thams cad kyi 'tshe bar gyur pa yin no//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang sbyin pa dang /tshul khrims dang /bzod pa dang /brtson 'grus dang /bsam gtan dang /shes rab 
56a1(1): @#//la sogs pa byang chub kyi tshogs rnams dang /phan pa 'gyur ba'i yo byad rnam pa du ma de dang de dag gis sems can thams cad kyi 'tsho bar gyur pa yin te/de ltar na byang chub sems   
56a2(1): dpa' sems can thams cad kyis 'tsho bar (gyur ba)yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' gnod pa dang /lan du phan 'dogs pa dang /bral ba yin zhe na/'di lta ste rigs kyi bu dper na sa chen po ni gnod pa(sa)
56a3(1): kyang zhe 'gras par 'gyur /phan pas kyang rjes su chags par mi 'gyur te/de la gnyis su 'du shes pa mi 'byung ngo //de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang gnod pa dag gis kyang zhe 'gras par mi 
56a4(1): 'gyur|phan pa dag gis kyang rjes su chags par ma 'gyur te/de ni sems can de dag la rjes su chags pa dang /khong khro ba yang mi skye ste/de ltar na byang chub sem dpa' gnod pa dang lan du phan 'dog+sa 
56a5(1): pa dang bral ba yin no//ji ltar na byang (   )chub sems dpa' chos kyi sprin chen po kun zed pa yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na sa chen po ni sprin chen po las bab pa'i chu'i klung chen po kun zer de//
56a6(1): bzod cing kun 'dzin to//de bzhin du byang chub (   )sems dpa' yang de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi sprin chen po las chos kyi 'ong ba chen po rab tu phab ba kun zed cing bzod pa 'dzin te/de ltar na byang chub (   )sem(sa)
56a7(1): dpa' chos kyi sprin chen(   ) kun zed pa yin no//ji ltar na byangachub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi gnas su gyur ba yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu (   )'di lta ste dper na/sa chen po ni sems can thams cad dang /
56b1(1): 'gro ba dang /'ong ba'i gnas su gyur pa yin no//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang sems can thams thams cad kyi bde 'gro dang /mya ngan las 'das par 'gro ba'i gnas su 'gyur ba yin/de lta na 
56b2(1): byang chub sems dpa' sems can thams cad kyi gnas su gyur pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i chos kyi sa bon gyi gzhir gyur ba yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/sa bon 
56b3(1): thams cad ni sa chen po gzhir gyur pa las skye'o//de bzhin du sems can thams cad kyidage ba'i chos kyi sa bon thams cad kyang byang chub sems dpa' gzhir gyur pa las skye ste/de ltar na byang chub 
56b4(1): sems dpa' dge ba'i chos ki sa bon gyi gzhir gyur pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub semasadapa' rin po che'i snod du gyur pa yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu'adi lta ste dper na sa chen po ni rin po che rnam pa sna 
56b5(1): tshogs kyi snod yin te/sa chen po la rin po che sna tshogs kun snang ngo//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang yon tan rin po che rnam pa sna tshogs kyi snod du gyur pa yin te/byang chub sems
56b6(1): dpa' la yon tan rin po che sna tshogs kunasnang ste/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' rin po che'i snod tu gyur pa yin no//ji lta na byang chub sems dpa'artsi chen po'i snod du gyur pa yin zhen//
56b7(1): rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/rtsi chen po gang dag nad rnam pa sna tshogs zhi bar gyur bdag kyang sa chen po la gnas te 'jig rten na snang ngo//de bzhin dugang dag gis nyon mongs pa'i nad rnam
57a1(1): @#//pa sna tshogs pa rnams zhi bar 'gyur ba'i chos kyi rtsi chen po rnams kyang byang chub sems dpa' la gnas shing 'jig rten du 'byung bar 'gyur te/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 
57a2(1): rtsi chen po'i snod du gyur pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi bsgul ba yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na sa chen po ni 'di ltar sha sbrang dang sbrang bu dang /rlung dang /nyi ma dang /
57a3(1): sbrul gyis reg pa'i sdug bsngal thams cad kyis mi bsgul ba yin no//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang 'di lta ste/sems can thams cad las byung ba'i phyir nang gi sdug bsngal thams 
57a4(1): cad kyis mi bsgul ba yinate/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi bsgul ba yin no //rigs kyi bu ji ltar na byang chub sems dba' mi sdang ba yin zhe na /rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na sa chen 
57a5(1): po ni glang po'i rgyal po dang /ri dgas kyi rgyal po rnam pa sna tshogs kyi sgra thos kyang mi skrag mi dngang dngang bar mi 'gyur ro//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' bdud dang mu stegs thams cad kyi 
57a6(1): sgra thos na mi skrag mi dngang dngang bar mi 'gyur te/rigs kyi bu de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' mi sdang ba yin no //rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na/byang chub sems dpa' sa 
57a7(1): chen po dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no//rigs kyi bu (chos)bcu dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa' chu dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no//bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/dge ba'i chos la gzhol ba rnams 
57b1(1): yin|dkar po'i chos thams cad bskyed pa rnams yin/dga' ba dang /dad pa dang /rangs pa'i gsher ba can rnams yin/nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun thams cad srung ba rnams yin/dangs 
57b2(1): zhing rnyog pa med de gsal ba rnams ba rnams yin/nyon mongs ba'i yongs su gdung ba rab tu zhi bar byed pa rnams yin/'dod pa la srid pa zlogs pa rnams pa yin/zab cing gting dpag dka' 
57b3(1): ba rnams yin/mtho dman 'gengs pa rnams yin/nyon mongs pa rdul thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa rnaMs yin no//rigs kyi bu 'di ltar na byang chub sems dge ba'i chos la gzhol ba yin   
57b4(1): zhe na|rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/chu ni gzhol bar 'gro ba dang /gzhol bar rgyu ba yin no//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang dge ba'i chos la gzhol zhing dge ba'i chos kyi phyir 
57b5(1): rgyu ba yin te/de ltar na byang chub sems pa' dge ba'i chos la gzhol pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems ngapa' dkar po'i (chos)thams cad bskyed pa yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste de pa(ra)
57b6(1): na chu ni rtswa dang /shing gel ba dang /rtsi dang nags tshal thams cad bskyed de/skyes pa rnams kyang phyir zhing rgyas par byed do//de bzhin du byang chub (   )sems dpa' yang ting nge 'dzin gyi chus byang chub 
57b7(1): kyi phyogs kyi chos thams cad rnam par bskyed do//skyes pa rnams kyang thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi shing du gyur gyi bar du phyir zhing rgyas par byed de//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyis shing du gyur pa
58a1(1): @#//dag gis sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 'bras bu rnam pa sna tshogs dag gis sems can rnams 'tsho bar 'gyur te/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dkar po'i chos thams cad   
58a2(1): bskyed pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems dnga' dga' ba dang /dad pa dang /rangs pa'i gsher ba can yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na chu ni ngo bo nyid kyis gsher ba can te/gzhan   
58a3(1): yang gsher bar byed do//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang ngo bo nyid kyis dga' ba dang /rangs pa'i gsher pa can yin te/sems can gzhan yang dga' ba dang /dad pa dang /rangs pa'i 
58a4(1): gsher ba can du byed do//de la dga' ba ni 'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos tshol ba'o //dad pa ni sangs rgyas dang /chos dang /dge 'dun la rten pa'o //rangs pa ni gang sems dag pa ste/de ltar 
58a5(1): na byang chub sems dpa' dga' ba dang /dang pa dang /rangs pa'i gshe ra ba can yin no//ji ltar na byangachub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun thams cad srung ba yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di  
58a6(1): 'di lta ste dper na/chu ni shing gi rnam pa dang /rtsa'i rnam pa'i rtsa ba dag gi/rgyun rnams srung lo//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang bsgom pa la gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi chus nyon mongs 
58a7(1): pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun thams cang sprul te/ji ltar nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun 'di dag gi ming yang shes pa dang /nyon mongs pa'i dri nga ba'i bag chags kyang med par 'gyur ba de lta bur sprul te/de ltar 
58b1(1): na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i rtsa ba'i rgyun thams cad sprul pa yin no//ji ltar na dang zhing /rnyog pa med de gsal ba yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na chu ni rang bzhin gyis dang zhing   
58b2(1): rnyogs pa med de gsal lo//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang rang bzhin gyis dang zhing rnyog pa med de gsal bayin no//de la bag la nyal ba dang kun nas ldang ba dang bral bas na dang ba'o//'dod 
58b3(1): chags dang zhe sdang dang /gti mug dang bral bas na rnyog pa med pa'o//dbang po rab tu dang bas ma gsal ba ste/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' dang zhing rnyog pa med do gsal ba yin no//ji lta na byang 
58b4(1): chub sems dpa' nyon mongasapa'i yongs su gdung ba rab tu zhi bar byed ce na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na chu ni sa chen po dpyid kyi tsha bas gdungs pa dang /de las gzhan yang sems can rnam+sa 
58b5(1): dbyid kyi tsha bar gdungs pa rnams rab tu zhi bar byed do//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang nyon mongs pas gdungs pa'i sems can gyi khams thams cad chos kyi chus rab tu zhi bar byed de//
58b6(1): de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pas yongs su gdungs pa rab tu zhi bar byed pa'o//ji ltar na byang chub sems dba' 'dod pa la srid pa bzlog pa yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper 
58b7(1): na chu yi skom pas nyen pa'i sems can rnams kyi skom pa rnam par bzlog go/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang yul la srid pas nyen pa'i sems can nyen par gyur pa dag la gang gis sem+sa 
59a1(1): @#//can de dag gi yul la sred pa ldog par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i chos kyi char mngon par 'bebs te/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' 'dod pa la sred pa bzlog par byed de//
59a2(1): ji ltar na byang chub sems dang zab cing gting dpag dka' ba yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/chu'i 'jings ni zab cing gting dpag dkar ba yin no//de bzhin du byang chub sem+sa 
59a3(1): dpa' ye shes yang dag par grub pa yang 'di lta ste/bdud thams cad dang /mu stegs can thams cad kyis gting dpag dka' zhing zab pa yin te/de ltar na byang chub kyi sems dpa' zab cing gting 
59a4(1): dpag bka' ba yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems pa' mtho dman 'gengs pa rnams yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu'adi lta ste dper na/chu yi klung ni sa'i phyogs mthon dman 'grengs so//de    
59a5(1): bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang chos kyi chu yi klung gis sems can gyi khams mthon dman 'gengs te//ji ltar chus 'gengs pa ltar sems can la gnod par byed pa de lta bu byang chub   
59a6(1): sems dpa' ma yin gyi/sems can de rnams la snying brtse ba'i phyir chos kyi chu yi klung 'bebs shing /de dag la gnod par mi byed do//de ltar byang chub sems dpa' mthon dman 'gengs
59a7(1): pa yin no//ji ltar na byangachub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i rdul thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed pa yin zhe na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na/chus ni sa'i phyogs skam zhing rdul 'thul ba    
59b1(1): thams cad snum par byed cing rdul de dag zhi bar byed do//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang sems can sems brtsub pa rnams 'di lta ste shes rab la gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi chus sem+sa
59b2(1): snum par byed cing sems can de dag gi nyon mongs pa'i rdul thams cad rab tu zhi bar byed do//de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i rdul tham sa cad rab tu zhi bar bye pa yin no//
59b3(1): rigs kyi bu chos bcu po de dag dang ldan na byang chub sems dpa'-i chu dang tshungs pa rnams yin no//rigs kyi bu chos bcu dang ldan na/byang chub sems dpa' me dang mtshungs pa rnams yin no 
59b4(1): bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/nyon mongs pa'i phyag dar khrod pa thams cad sreg par byed/sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams kyang yongs su smin par byed /nyon mongs pa'i 'dam thams cad kyang skam  
59b5(1): par byed /me'i phung po dang 'dra ba rnams yin/smad bar gyur ba rnams yin/skrag par byed pa rnams yin/dbugs 'byin pa rnams yin/sems can thams cad kyi thun mong du gyur pa rnams  
59b6(1): yin|mchod bar byed pa rnams yin/mi brnyas par byed pa rnams yin/rigs kyi bu de la ji ltar na byang chub sems dang pa' nyon mongs pa'i phyag dar khung thams cad sreg ce na rigs kyi bu 'di  
59b7(1): lta ste dper na me ni phyag dar khrod dang rtswa dang /shing dang /gel ba dang /rtsi dang /sdong po thams cad sreg go/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang shes rab kyis bag la nyal ba dang /kun n+sa 
60a1(1): @#//ldang ba dang /'dod chags dang /zhe sdang dang /gti mug gi nyon mongs pa'i phyag dar khung thams cad sreg ste/de ltar na byang chub sems dpa' nyon mongs pa'i phyag dar lung 
60a2(1): thams cad sreg pa yin no//ji ltar na byang chub sems pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yongs su smin par byed ce na/rigs kyi bu 'di lta ste dper na me ni lo tog dang /rtsi dang sdong po'i rnam pa 
60a3(1): thams cad yongs su sminapar byed/de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang shes rab kyis nang gi sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams yongs su smin par byedapa'ang ji ltar 'jig par mi 'gyur ba de ltar yongs su

306b1(1): snyigs ma dang /tshe'i snyigs ma'i tshe 'jig rten gyi khams mi 'jed du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas te/'jig rten thams cad 
306b2(1): dang mi mthun pa'i chos bstan pa ni ngo tshar legs so//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal ba/shA ri'i bu snyigs ma lnga'i tshe 'jig rten du ngas 'jig rten gyi 
306b3(1): khams mi 'jed du/bla na med pa yang dag parardzogs pa'i byang chub mngon
306b4(1): kyis de skad ces bka' stsal nas/tshe dang ldan shA ri'i bu dang/nyan thos chen po de dag dang/byang
306b5(1): chub sems dpa' de dag dang/lha dang mi dang /lha ma yin dang /dri zar bcas pa'i 'jig rten yid rangs ste/bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs la bar mngon par stong ngo//'phags
306b6(1): pa bde ba can gyi bkod pa zhes bya ba theg pa chen po'i mdo rdzogs s+ho////@#/rgya gar skad du/swa sti ya na gA thA/bod skad du/bde legs su 'gyur ba tshigs su bcad pa/thams cad mkhyen pa la phyag 'tshal lo/
306b7(1): 'jig rten 'dren pa'i drung du ni/lha zhig nye bar song nas su/gang gis kun du bde legs pa/bdagi gas zhu yis bcom ldan gsung /dam pa de dag gsan pa dang /'jig rten 'dren pas lung bstan pa/gang gis
